One call cost $0.013384; simulation and scored run remain unchanged. Proposed tests are not yet executed or accepted. The full repair/generalization goal remains active.

### Implemented constructor and strict evidence boundary

New `src/weft/shared_world_support.py` exposes
`construct_static_support_run(body_source, config, out, *, max_seconds=30)`;
the ordinary CLI is `shared-support-build --body-source ... --config ... --out ...`.
Config profile `shared-static-support-construction-v1` requires exact original
video/body-manifest hashes, scale, 1–12 static box/sphere/compound objects,
support requirements and an explicit root-assistance boolean (normally false).
Support provenance must be authored, uncalibrated, contact-unverified and
physically unverified. False observed/calibrated claims and unknown fields reject.

The constructor reuses the existing placement scaffold, removes only its
ground-only inventory and two incompatible assumptions, then validates v4.
It never invokes person-only admission or invents an inventory proof. Human
bindings name the exact original observation document; added supports have
null observation identities. Every declared body-source member and supplied
file-config byte is copied unchanged. All original slots, missing observations,
native timestamps, references, first pose, roots and floor are preserved.
Separate `exercise-task.json` uses the fixed support-task gates and exact scene.
Reference feasibility is informational; lab admission still checks initial geometry.

### Direct mobile body to shared equipment

Use `shared-exercise-build --human-run <sealed-mobile-run-or-lab> --body-scale 1.0 --full-reference`, with the existing sealed equipment export, explicit camera/equipment configuration and any source mappings. `--run` remains the mutually exclusive existing-shared-world route; it cannot accept `--body-scale`. [Complete command](../equipment-proposals.md).

`prepare_shared_world_seed` reuses the legacy adapter's pure scene arithmetic, without running its demo-prop probes or reference renderer. The `body_seed/` snapshot preserves exact human/evidence/media bytes and canonical observation bytes, source slots and clocks. It retains the source manifest; unrelated derived training/preview files are verified but not duplicated, so keep the immutable original mobile run. Its ball/pedestal template is explicitly unexposed construction scaffolding; the normal constructor replaces those objects with selected source equipment. Both lab importers reject the scaffold. No decoder/model/API call is made. It does not automate equipment selection, camera calibration or shape assumptions.

### Common runtime qualification — verified

[`weft qualify`](../runtime-qualification.md) adds one capability-driven protocol for rolling, planar, spatial, human, imitation, feet, shared-world and human-only shared-exercise runtimes. It freezes input/config/runtime identities, preserves each original task and interval, journals actions, checks fresh seeded exact replay and a matched changed-command prefix. It never executes/trains a learned policy or changes scorers. Operational completion, task success and physical validation stay separate. Limits: five cases /120 s /12,000 total steps /128 MiB hard ceilings; defaults 60 s /64 MiB, one worker.

Independent review fixed final-step returned engine failures being mistaken for completion, unpinned consumed outer lab metadata, recording-capacity checks after physics, malformed runtime pins, initial deadline publication and unstarted-recorder cleanup. **61 distinct latest-passing tests**, `.local/runtime-qualification-tests-summary-01.json`, include 53 runner and eight CLI boundary cases. ### Direct body preparation — verified

`weft exercise-body` prepares `exercise-body-source-v1` from raw video plus an explicit pinned local pose model, or reuses a sealed pose run with `--evidence-run`. Existing `shared-exercise-build --human-run` accepts this package directly. Source packages contain exact video/poses, model/options, canonical observations, sampled native clocks and a mobile body, with **no fabricated scene/task and no intermediate body lab/training**. Defaults remain video-mode/raw identity; saved pose options are inherited without overrides. Original run configuration is explicitly separate from the new mobile construction. [Commands and artifact guide](../equipment-proposals.md#prepare-body-evidence-without-a-lab).

`exercise_body_source.py` owns bounded encoding/persistence. Optional writer hooks in `exercise_vision.py` and `human.py` preserve old serialized outputs and pose/physics logic. The narrow seed branch pins/rechecks the current validator and rejects failure markers before and after consumption. Original inputs cannot be replaced by the sink.
